- **realmarketapi-market-snapshot**: Summarises every symbol on the plan in one call with hourly and daily change. - **realmarketapi-moving-average-analysis**: Reads SMA, EMA and WMA values, their slopes, stacking and crossovers. - **realmarketapi-multi-timeframe**: Checks trend alignment from the highest to the lowest timeframe in one pass. Use when the user asks for a top-down view or whether timeframes agree.
